# Logion 113

- **Witness status:** Coptic-only
- **Parallels:** PENDING ROBERT
- **State:** DRAFTED (G1 self-audit done — see notes)

## Greek

No Greek witness — Coptic alone.

## Coptic (Coptic Scriptorium `thomas.gospel` v6.2.0, CC BY 4.0; base sentences s0381–s0385)

ⲡⲉϫⲁⲩ ⲛⲁϥ ⲛϭⲓⲛⲉϥⲙⲁⲑⲏⲧⲏⲥ ϫⲉⲧⲙⲛⲧⲣⲣⲟ ⲉⲥⲛⲏⲩ ⲛⲁϣ ⲛϩⲟⲟⲩ

ⲉⲥⲛ̄ⲛⲏⲩ ⲁⲛ ϩⲛⲟⲩϭⲱϣⲧ ⲉⲃⲟⲗ

ⲉⲩⲛⲁϫⲟⲟⲥ ⲁⲛ ϫⲉⲉⲓⲥϩⲏⲏⲧⲉ ⲙⲡⲓⲥⲁ ⲏ ⲉⲓⲥϩⲏⲏⲧⲉ ⲧⲏ

ⲁⲗⲗⲁ ⲧⲙⲛⲧⲣⲣⲟ ⲙⲡⲉⲓⲱⲧ ⲉⲥⲡⲟⲣϣ ⲉⲃⲟⲗ ϩⲓϫⲙⲡⲕⲁϩ

ⲁⲩⲱ ⲛⲣⲱⲙⲉ ⲛⲁⲩ ⲁⲛ ⲉⲣⲟⲥ

## English draft

His disciples said to him: "On what day is the kingdom coming?"

"It is not coming by watching. They will not say: 'Look, here' or 'Look, there.' But the kingdom of the Father is spread out over the earth — and people do not see it."

## Notes

- ⲡⲉϫⲁⲩ ⲛⲁϥ ⲛϭⲓⲛⲉϥⲙⲁⲑⲏⲧⲏⲥ = "they said to him, by his disciples" — the agentive ⲛϭⲓ construction rendered as the plain subject.
- "On what day" renders ⲛⲁϣ ⲛϩⲟⲟⲩ — uniform with Logion 88's ⲁϣ ⲛϩⲟⲟⲩ.
- "by watching" renders ϩⲛⲟⲩϭⲱϣⲧ ⲉⲃⲟⲗ — ⲥⲱϣⲧ (watch/observe); fork "by observation" named, not chosen.
- ⲙⲡⲓⲥⲁ / ⲧⲏ = "this side / that side" — rendered "here/there," the plain floor.
- "is spread out" renders ⲉⲥⲡⲟⲣϣ ⲉⲃⲟⲗ (stative) — the kingdom's extent is a present state, kept as written.
- "kingdom of the Father" — the only "kingdom of the Father" phrasing in the slice beside the plain "kingdom" elsewhere; kept exactly, not aligned to any other wording.
- ⲁⲩⲱ ⲛⲣⲱⲙⲉ = "and people" — manuscript reads ⲣ̄ⲣⲱⲙⲉ (ⲁⲩⲱ ⲣ̄ⲣⲱⲙⲉ); the normalized edition reads ⲛⲣⲱⲙⲉ and the draft follows it, with the manuscript reading flagged here.
- The double negative shape (not-by-watching / not-saying-here-or-there / but-spread-out-and-unseen) is kept as the saying's own logic — no explaining clause added.
- No Greek printed or invented; Coptic alone, so stated.
- No doublets in this slice to merge.
- No parallel citations added (PENDING ROBERT).
- G1: checked against base sentences s0381–s0385 (chapter division ch.113, five sentences); quoted Coptic verified token-for-token against the base normalized text this run; manuscript-layer flag (ⲣ̄ⲣⲱⲙⲉ) verified in the orig layer; no modern translation consulted or quoted.

# Logion 114

- **Witness status:** Coptic-only
- **Parallels:** PENDING ROBERT
- **State:** DRAFTED (G1 self-audit done — see notes)

## Greek

No Greek witness — Coptic alone.

## Coptic (Coptic Scriptorium `thomas.gospel` v6.2.0, CC BY 4.0; base sentences s0386–s0388)

ⲡⲉϫⲉⲥⲓⲙⲱⲛ ⲡⲉⲧⲣⲟⲥ ⲛⲁⲩ ϫⲉⲙⲁⲣⲉⲙⲁⲣⲓϩⲁⲙ ⲉⲓ ⲉⲃⲟⲗ ⲛϩⲏⲧⲛ ϫⲉⲛⲥϩⲓⲟⲙⲉ ⲙⲡϣⲁ ⲁⲛ ⲙⲡⲱⲛϩ

ⲡⲉϫⲉⲓⲏⲥⲟⲩⲥ ϫⲉⲉⲓⲥϩⲏⲏⲧⲉ ⲁⲛⲟⲕ ϯⲛⲁⲥⲱⲕ ⲙⲙⲟⲥ ϫⲉⲕⲁⲁⲥ ⲉⲓⲛⲁⲁⲁⲥ ⲛϩⲟⲟⲩⲧ ϩⲓⲛⲁ ⲉⲥⲛⲁϣⲱⲡⲉ ϩⲱⲱⲥ ⲛⲟⲩⲡⲛⲉⲩⲙⲁ ⲉϥⲟⲛϩ ⲉϥⲉⲓⲛⲉ ⲙⲙⲱⲧⲛ ⲛϩⲟⲟⲩⲧ

ϫⲉⲥϩⲓⲙⲉ ⲛⲓⲙ ⲉⲥⲛⲁⲁⲁⲥ ⲛϩⲟⲟⲩⲧ ⲥⲛⲁⲃⲱⲕ ⲉϩⲟⲩⲛ ⲉⲧⲙⲛⲧⲣⲣⲟ ⲛⲙⲡⲏⲩⲉ

## English draft

Simon Peter said to them: "Let Mary leave us, for women are not worthy of life."

Jesus said: "Look — I myself will guide her, so as to make her male, so that she too may become a living spirit, like you men. For every woman who makes herself male will enter the kingdom of heaven."

## Notes

- "not worthy of life" renders ⲙⲡϣⲁ ⲁⲛ ⲙⲡⲱⲛϩ — the ⲙⲡϣⲁ construction kept uniform with Logion 111 ("not worthy of him").
- ⲁⲛⲟⲕ ϯⲛⲁⲥⲱⲕ ⲙⲙⲟⲥ = "I — I will guide her" — the emphatic pronoun kept ("I myself"); ⲥⲱⲕ (draw/pull/guide) fork named on the page ("draw her" not chosen).
- "make her male" renders ⲁⲁⲥ ⲛϩⲟⲟⲩⲧ; "every woman who makes herself male" renders ⲥϩⲓⲙⲉ ⲛⲓⲙ ⲉⲥⲛⲁⲁⲁⲥ ⲛϩⲟⲟⲩⲧ — the hard saying is left literal and unsolved, in the same spirit as Logion 105's hard saying (slice 21). Robert's resonance stays reader-lens only; it does not soften or redirect a word here.
- "living spirit" renders ⲟⲩⲡⲛⲉⲩⲙⲁ ⲉϥⲟⲛϩ (πνεῦμα); "like you men" renders ⲉϥⲉⲓⲛⲉ ⲙⲙⲱⲧⲛ ⲛϩⲟⲟⲩⲧ — ⲉⲓⲛⲉ "likeness" kept uniform with Logia 83/84, distinct from ϩⲓⲕⲱⲛ "image."
- "kingdom of heaven" renders ⲧⲙⲛⲧⲣⲣⲟ ⲛⲙⲡⲏⲩⲉ — the plural "heavens" kept; not aligned to "kingdom of the Father" in Logion 113, each kept as written.
- Speaker handoffs kept: Peter's "them" (the disciples) and Jesus' "you men" are not merged or glossed.
- Manuscript colophon, not part of the saying: after this logion the manuscript carries ⲡⲉⲩⲁⲅⲅⲉⲗⲓⲟⲛ ⲡⲕⲁⲧⲁ ⲑⲱⲙⲁⲥ ("the gospel according to Thomas") — recorded here, not translated as Logion 114 material.
- No Greek printed or invented; Coptic alone, so stated.
- No doublets in this slice to merge.
- No parallel citations added (PENDING ROBERT).
- G1: checked against base sentences s0386–s0388 (chapter division ch.114, three sentences); quoted Coptic verified token-for-token against the base normalized text this run; no modern translation consulted or quoted.
